Cup 2005/06


Legend:
L1 	= Ligue 1 (Level 1)
L2 	= Ligue 2 (Level 2)
Nat 	= National (Level 3)
CFA	= Championnat de France Amateur (Level 4)
CFA2	= Championnat de France Amateur 2 (Level 5)
DH	= Division d`Honnor (Level 6)
DHR	= Division d`Honnor Régional (Level 7)
DSR	= Division Supérieur Régional (Level 8)
PH	= Promotion d`Honnor (Level 9)
PL	= Promotion de Ligue (Level 10)
LI	= Ligue Interrégional (Level 10)
PI	= Promotion Interdistrict (Level 10)
Dis	= District Levels (Level 11 to 18)

12.Nantes 2		77 34 12  7 15	46-48 [ 3  0  1  1  0- 1]

14.Bordeaux 2		74 34  9 13 12	31-40 [ 3  0  1  1  0- 2]

18.Blois		62 34  6 10 18	38-60 Relegated

NB: The game Yzeure-Orleáns was scheduled for Mar 4, at 18:00; on Mar 3,
    the city council decided to close the stadium of Yzeure, however the 
    announcement occurred after 12:00, too late for the FFF to postpone
    the game.  On Mar 4, the referee and Orléans entered the field, which
    was considered to be in good condition; Yzeure did not show and on
    18:30 the referee awarded the match 3-0 to Orléans.
    Yzeure protested, but always lost the case, also on various appeals.
    However, after the end of the championship, the FFF suddenly
    decided to reschedule the match, to the great chagrin of Rodez
    Aveyron who had secured the championship and promotion but stood to
    lose both in case Yzeure would win the rescheduled match.
    On Jun 3, Orleáns did not show, protesting its rescheduling, upon
    which FFF awarded the match 3-0 to Yzeure and deducted 4 points from
    Orléans.

Championship Reserves

Semifinals
[May 30]
Lyon		1 - 0   Toulouse
Rennes		3 - 2   Auxerre

Final
[Jun 3]
Lyon		3 - 1   Rennes

Lyon is the champions of Championship Reserves



Fifth Level (CFA 2)


- Top team of each group is promoted to CFA.
- Reserve teams of clubs of Ligue 2 or National can't be promoted to CFA.
- The second places enter in the "Second place table".
- The last 3 of each group are relegated to DH.
- The 13th of each group, the last 12th placed of "12th place table" and 
  the 2 best 14h placed of "14th place table" enter in the Relegation 
  Playoff with the 22 runners-up in the DH.
